<script id="mfn-dnmc-custom-js">
//<![CDATA[
function menuCustomScroll() {
    let scrollOffset = window.pageYOffset ;
    if (scrollOffset > 200) {
        document.getElementById("top-menu-custom").style.background = "#775ada";
    }
    else {
        document.getElementById("top-menu-custom").style.background = "transparent";
    }
}
document.addEventListener("scroll", menuCustomScroll , true);
//]]>
</script>
